#ifndef RRAY_ITERATOR_H
#define RRAY_ITERATOR_H

#include "dimensionality.h"
#include "rlang.h"

#include "decl/iterator-decl.h"

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Core "next" algorithm used by all iterators
//
// Takes one step along the multidimensional point. Calls `STEP` and `RESET`
// hooks, which are what define each iterator.
#define RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(IT, STEP, RESET)                                    \
  (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                                  \
                                                                               \
  for (int i = 1; i < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++i) {                       \
    ++(IT)->v_point[i];                                                        \
                                                                               \
    if ((IT)->v_point[i] < (IT)->v_point_dimensions[i]) {                      \
      STEP return;                                                             \
    }                                                                          \
                                                                               \
    (IT)->v_point[i] = 0;                                                      \
                                                                               \
    RESET                                                                      \
  }

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Simplest iterator
//
// Walks the multidimensional point space, providing access to the current
// multidimensional point
struct rray_point_iterator {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;
};

static inline void rray_point_iterator_init(
  struct rray_point_iterator* it,
  r_ssize size,
  const int* v_point_dimensions,
  int point_dimensionality
) {
  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);

  it->index = 0;
  it->size = size;

  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;

  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
  }
  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);
}

static inline bool rray_point_iterator_finished(
  const struct rray_point_iterator* it
) {
  return it->index == it->size;
}

static inline void rray_point_iterator_next(struct rray_point_iterator* it) {
  RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(it, {}, {})
}

#define RRAY_POINT_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, POINT, ...)                    \
  do {                                                                         \
    struct rray_point_iterator* rray_it_ = (IT);                               \
    int rray_point_[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];                                  \
    const int* POINT = rray_point_;                                            \
    while (!rray_point_iterator_finished(rray_it_)) {                          \
      const r_ssize rray_end_ =                                                \
        rray_it_->index + rray_it_->v_point_dimensions[0];                     \
      memcpy(                                                                  \
        rray_point_,                                                           \
        rray_it_->v_point,                                                     \
        sizeof(int) * rray_it_->point_dimensionality                           \
      );                                                                       \
      for (r_ssize INDEX = rray_it_->index; INDEX != rray_end_;                \
           ++INDEX, ++rray_point_[0]) {                                        \
        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
      }                                                                        \
      rray_point_iterator_next(rray_it_);                                      \
    }                                                                          \
  } while (0)

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Broadcasting iterator
//
// Walks the multidimensional point space. Reports a 1D `location` in an
// alternate subspace.
//
// For broadcasting, the dimensions you broadcast to make up the larger point
// space. This is walked in order. The original dimensions of the array make
// up the subspace. So as you walk the output's point space you can fetch
// `location`s back into your original array to pull from.
//
// For reducing, it's actually a special form of broadcasting. The original
// dimensions of the array are the point space. The reduced dimensions are the
// subspace. So as you walk the original array, you can fetch `location`s into
// the output to accumulate the reduced result at.
struct rray_iterator {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;

  r_ssize location;
  r_ssize v_location_strides[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
};

static inline void rray_iterator_init(
  struct rray_iterator* it,
  r_ssize size,
  const int* v_point_dimensions,
  int point_dimensionality,
  const int* v_location_dimensions,
  int location_dimensionality
) {
  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);

  it->index = 0;
  it->size = size;

  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;

  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
  }
  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);

  rray__location_strides_init(
    it->v_location_strides,
    v_point_dimensions,
    point_dimensionality,
    v_location_dimensions,
    location_dimensionality,
    "location"
  );
  it->location = 0;
}

static inline bool rray_iterator_finished(const struct rray_iterator* it) {
  return it->index == it->size;
}

static inline void rray_iterator_next(struct rray_iterator* it) {
  RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(
    it,
    { it->location += it->v_location_strides[i]; },
    {
      it->location -=
        (it->v_point_dimensions[i] - 1) * it->v_location_strides[i];
    }
  )
}

#define RRAY_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, LOCATION, ...)                       \
  do {                                                                         \
    struct rray_iterator* rray_it_ = (IT);                                     \
    while (!rray_iterator_finished(rray_it_)) {                                \
      const r_ssize rray_end_ =                                                \
        rray_it_->index + rray_it_->v_point_dimensions[0];                     \
      const r_ssize rray_stride_ = rray_it_->v_location_strides[0];            \
      for (r_ssize INDEX = rray_it_->index, LOCATION = rray_it_->location;     \
           INDEX != rray_end_;                                                 \
           ++INDEX, LOCATION += rray_stride_) {                                \
        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
      }                                                                        \
      rray_iterator_next(rray_it_);                                            \
    }                                                                          \
  } while (0)

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Same as `rray_iterator`, but reports in two location spaces while only
// walking the point space once
struct rray_iterator2 {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;

+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import sys
+
+p = Path('~/files/r/packages/rray4/src/iterator.h')
+s = p.read_text()
+n = int(sys.argv[1])
+
+def replace_macro(name, body):
+    global s
+    start = s.index('#define ' + name + '(')
+    end = s.index('\n', s.index('  } while (0)', start))
+    s = s[:start] + ' \\\n'.join(body.strip().splitlines()) + s[end:]
+
+def get_macro(name):
+    start = s.index('#define ' + name + '(')
+    end = s.index('\n', s.index('  } while (0)', start))
+    return '\n'.join(re.sub(r'\s*\\$', '', line) for line in s[start:end].splitlines())
+
+def remove_function(signature):
+    global s
+    start = s.index(signature)
+    end = s.index('\n}\n', start) + 3
+    s = s[:start] + s[end:].lstrip('\n')
+
+if n <= 5:
+    name = 'RRAY_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH'
+    m = get_macro(name)
+    if n == 1:
+        m = m.replace('    struct rray_iterator* rray_it_ = (IT);\n', '')
+        m = m.replace('rray_it_', '(IT)')
+    if n == 2:
+        m = m.replace('!rray_iterator_finished((IT))', '(IT)->index != (IT)->size')
+        remove_function('static inline bool rray_iterator_finished(')
+    if n == 3:
+        m = m.replace('rray_end_', 'end').replace('rray_stride_', 'stride')
+    if n == 4:
+        m = m.replace('      rray_iterator_next((IT));', '''      (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];
+      for (int axis = 1; axis < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++axis) {
+        ++(IT)->v_point[axis];
+        if ((IT)->v_point[axis] < (IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis]) {
+          (IT)->location += (IT)->v_location_strides[axis];
+          break;
+        }
+        (IT)->v_point[axis] = 0;
+        (IT)->location -= (IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis] * (IT)->v_location_strides[axis] - (IT)->v_location_strides[axis];
+      }''')
+        m = m.replace('(IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis] * (IT)->v_location_strides[axis] - (IT)->v_location_strides[axis]', '((IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis] - 1) * (IT)->v_location_strides[axis]')
+        remove_function('static inline void rray_iterator_next(')
+    if n == 5:
+        m = re.sub(r'      for \(r_ssize INDEX = \(IT\)->index, LOCATION = \(IT\)->location;\s*INDEX != end;\s*\+\+INDEX, LOCATION \+= stride\) \{', '''      r_ssize LOCATION = (IT)->location;
+      for (r_ssize INDEX = (IT)->index; INDEX != end; ++INDEX) {''', m)
+        m = m.replace('        __VA_ARGS__\n', '        __VA_ARGS__\n        LOCATION += stride;\n')
+    replace_macro(name, m)
+
+if n == 6:
+    remove_function('static inline bool rray_iterator2_finished(')
+    remove_function('static inline void rray_iterator2_next(')
+    replace_macro('RRAY_ITERATOR2_FOR_EACH', '''#define RRAY_ITERATOR2_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, ...)
+  do {
+    while ((IT)->index != (IT)->size) {
+      const r_ssize end = (IT)->index + (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];
+      const r_ssize stride1 = (IT)->v_location1_strides[0];
+      const r_ssize stride2 = (IT)->v_location2_strides[0];
+      r_ssize LOCATION1 = (IT)->location1;
+      r_ssize LOCATION2 = (IT)->location2;
+      for (r_ssize INDEX = (IT)->index; INDEX != end; ++INDEX) {
+        __VA_ARGS__
+        LOCATION1 += stride1;
+        LOCATION2 += stride2;
+      }
+      (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];
+      for (int axis = 1; axis < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++axis) {
+        ++(IT)->v_point[axis];

+    replace_macro('RRAY_POINT_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH', m)
+
+p.write_text(s)

     9	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	
    11	// Core "next" algorithm used by all iterators
    12	//
    13	// Takes one step along the multidimensional point. Calls `STEP` and `RESET`
    14	// hooks, which are what define each iterator.
    15	#define RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(IT, STEP, RESET)                                    \
    16	  (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                                  \
    17	                                                                               \
    18	  for (int i = 1; i < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++i) {                       \
    19	    ++(IT)->v_point[i];                                                        \
    20	                                                                               \
    21	    if ((IT)->v_point[i] < (IT)->v_point_dimensions[i]) {                      \
    22	      STEP return;                                                             \
    23	    }                                                                          \
    24	                                                                               \
    25	    (IT)->v_point[i] = 0;                                                      \
    26	                                                                               \
    27	    RESET                                                                      \
    28	  }
    29	
    30	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31	
    32	// Simplest iterator
    33	//
    34	// Walks the multidimensional point space, providing access to the current
    35	// multidimensional point
    36	struct rray_point_iterator {
    37	  r_ssize index;
    38	  r_ssize size;
    39	
    40	  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
    41	  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
    42	  int point_dimensionality;
    43	};
    44	
    45	static inline void rray_point_iterator_init(
    46	  struct rray_point_iterator* it,
    47	  r_ssize size,
    48	  const int* v_point_dimensions,
    49	  int point_dimensionality
    50	) {
    51	  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);
    52	
    53	  it->index = 0;
    54	  it->size = size;
    55	
    56	  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;
    57	
    58	  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    59	    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
    60	  }
    61	  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);
    62	}
    63	
    64	static inline bool rray_point_iterator_finished(
    65	  const struct rray_point_iterator* it
    66	) {
    67	  return it->index == it->size;
    68	}
    69	
    70	static inline void rray_point_iterator_next(struct rray_point_iterator* it) {
    71	  RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(it, {}, {})
    72	}
    73	
    74	#define RRAY_POINT_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, POINT, ...)                    \
    75	  do {                                                                         \
    76	    struct rray_point_iterator* rray_it_ = (IT);                               \
    77	    int rray_point_[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];                                  \
    78	    const int* POINT = rray_point_;                                            \
    79	    while (!rray_point_iterator_finished(rray_it_)) {                          \

    81	        rray_it_->index + rray_it_->v_point_dimensions[0];                     \
    82	      memcpy(                                                                  \

    85	        sizeof(int) * rray_it_->point_dimensionality                           \

    90	      }                                                                        \

    92	    }                                                                          \
    93	  } while (0)
    94	
    95	// --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96	
    97	// Broadcasting iterator
    98	//
    99	// Walks the multidimensional point space. Reports a 1D `location` in an
   100	// alternate subspace.
   101	//
   102	// For broadcasting, the dimensions you broadcast to make up the larger point
   103	// space. This is walked in order. The original dimensions of the array make
   104	// up the subspace. So as you walk the output's point space you can fetch
   105	// `location`s back into your original array to pull from.
   106	//
   107	// For reducing, it's actually a special form of broadcasting. The original
   108	// dimensions of the array are the point space. The reduced dimensions are the
   109	// subspace. So as you walk the original array, you can fetch `location`s into
   110	// the output to accumulate the reduced result at.
   111	struct rray_iterator {
   112	  r_ssize index;
   113	  r_ssize size;
   114	
   115	  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
   116	  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
   117	  int point_dimensionality;
   118	
   119	  r_ssize location;
   120	  r_ssize v_location_strides[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
   121	};
   122	
   123	static inline void rray_iterator_init(
   124	  struct rray_iterator* it,
   125	  r_ssize size,
   126	  const int* v_point_dimensions,
   127	  int point_dimensionality,
   128	  const int* v_location_dimensions,
   129	  int location_dimensionality
   130	) {
   131	  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);
   132	
   133	  it->index = 0;
   134	  it->size = size;
   135	
   136	  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;
   137	
   138	  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
   139	    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
   140	  }
   141	  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);
   142	
   143	  rray__location_strides_init(
   144	    it->v_location_strides,
   145	    v_point_dimensions,
   146	    point_dimensionality,
   147	    v_location_dimensions,
   148	    location_dimensionality,
   149	    "location"
   150	  );
   151	  it->location = 0;
   152	}
   153	
   154	static inline void rray_iterator_next(struct rray_iterator* it) {
   155	  RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(
   156	    it,
   157	    { it->location += it->v_location_strides[i]; },
   158	    {
   159	      it->location -=
   160	        (it->v_point_dimensions[i] - 1) * it->v_location_strides[i];
   161	    }
   162	  )
   163	}
   164	
   165	#define RRAY_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, LOCATION, ...)                       \
   166	  do {                                                                         \
   167	    while ((IT)->index != (IT)->size) {                                        \
   168	      const r_ssize end = (IT)->index + (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];           \
   169	      const r_ssize stride = (IT)->v_location_strides[0];                      \
   170	      for (r_ssize INDEX = (IT)->index, LOCATION = (IT)->location;             \
   171	           INDEX != end;                                                       \
   172	           ++INDEX, LOCATION += stride) {                                      \
   173	        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
   174	      }                                                                        \
   175	      rray_iterator_next((IT));                                                \
   176	    }                                                                          \
   177	  } while (0)

#ifndef RRAY_ITERATOR_H
#define RRAY_ITERATOR_H

#include "dimensionality.h"
#include "rlang.h"

#include "decl/iterator-decl.h"

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Core "next" algorithm used by all iterators
//
// Takes one step along the multidimensional point. Calls `STEP` and `RESET`
// hooks, which are what define each iterator.
#define RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(IT, STEP, RESET)                                    \
  (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                                  \
                                                                               \
  for (int i = 1; i < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++i) {                       \
    ++(IT)->v_point[i];                                                        \
                                                                               \
    if ((IT)->v_point[i] < (IT)->v_point_dimensions[i]) {                      \
      STEP return;                                                             \
    }                                                                          \
                                                                               \
    (IT)->v_point[i] = 0;                                                      \
                                                                               \
    RESET                                                                      \
  }

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Simplest iterator
//
// Walks the multidimensional point space, providing access to the current
// multidimensional point
struct rray_point_iterator {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;
};

static inline void rray_point_iterator_init(
  struct rray_point_iterator* it,
  r_ssize size,
  const int* v_point_dimensions,
  int point_dimensionality
) {
  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);

  it->index = 0;
  it->size = size;

  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;

  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
  }
  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);
}

static inline bool rray_point_iterator_finished(
  const struct rray_point_iterator* it
) {
  return it->index == it->size;
}

static inline void rray_point_iterator_next(struct rray_point_iterator* it){
  RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT(it, {}, {})
}

#define RRAY_POINT_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, POINT, ...)                    \
  do {                                                                         \
    struct rray_point_iterator* rray_it_ = (IT);                               \
    int rray_point_[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];                                  \
    const int* POINT = rray_point_;                                            \
    while (!rray_point_iterator_finished(rray_it_)) {                          \
      const r_ssize rray_end_ =                                                \
        rray_it_->index + rray_it_->v_point_dimensions[0];                     \
      memcpy(                                                                  \
        rray_point_,                                                           \
        rray_it_->v_point,                                                     \
        sizeof(int) * rray_it_->point_dimensionality                           \
      );                                                                       \
      for (r_ssize INDEX = rray_it_->index; INDEX != rray_end_;                \
           ++INDEX, ++rray_point_[0]) {                                        \
        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
      }                                                                        \
      rray_point_iterator_next(rray_it_);                                      \
    }                                                                          \
  } while (0)

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Broadcasting iterator
//
// Walks the multidimensional point space. Reports a 1D `location` in an
// alternate subspace.
//
// For broadcasting, the dimensions you broadcast to make up the larger point
// space. This is walked in order. The original dimensions of the array make
// up the subspace. So as you walk the output's point space you can fetch
// `location`s back into your original array to pull from.
  r_ssize stride = 1;
  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    const int dimension =
      (i < location_dimensionality) ? v_location_dimensions[i] : 1;
    v_location_strides[i] = (dimension == 1) ? 0 : stride;
    stride *= dimension;
  }
}

#undef RRAY_ITERATOR_NEXT

#endif

#define RRAY_POINT_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, POINT, ...)                    \

        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
        ++POINT[0];                                                            \
      }                                                                        \

      (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                              \
                                                                               \

        ++(IT)->v_point[axis];                                                 \

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Broadcasting iterator
//
// Walks the multidimensional point space. Reports a 1D `location` in an
// alternate subspace.
//
// For broadcasting, the dimensions you broadcast to make up the larger point
// space. This is walked in order. The original dimensions of the array make
// up the subspace. So as you walk the output's point space you can fetch
// `location`s back into your original array to pull from.
//
// For reducing, it's actually a special form of broadcasting. The original
// dimensions of the array are the point space. The reduced dimensions are the
// subspace. So as you walk the original array, you can fetch `location`s into
// the output to accumulate the reduced result at.
struct rray_iterator {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;

  r_ssize location;
  r_ssize v_location_strides[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
};

static inline void rray_iterator_init(
  struct rray_iterator* it,
  r_ssize size,
  const int* v_point_dimensions,
  int point_dimensionality,
  const int* v_location_dimensions,
  int location_dimensionality
) {
  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);

  it->index = 0;
  it->size = size;

  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;

  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
  }
  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);

  rray__location_strides_init(
    it->v_location_strides,
    v_point_dimensions,
    point_dimensionality,
    v_location_dimensions,
    location_dimensionality,
    "location"
  );
  it->location = 0;
}

#define RRAY_ITERATOR_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, LOCATION, ...)                       \
  do {                                                                         \
    while ((IT)->index != (IT)->size) {                                        \
      const r_ssize end = (IT)->index + (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];           \
      const r_ssize stride = (IT)->v_location_strides[0];                      \
      r_ssize LOCATION = (IT)->location;                                       \
      for (r_ssize INDEX = (IT)->index; INDEX != end; ++INDEX) {               \
        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
        LOCATION += stride;                                                    \
      }                                                                        \
      (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                              \
                                                                               \
      for (int axis = 1; axis < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++axis) {          \
        ++(IT)->v_point[axis];                                                 \
                                                                               \
        if ((IT)->v_point[axis] < (IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis]) {            \
          (IT)->location += (IT)->v_location_strides[axis];                    \
          break;                                                               \
        }                                                                      \
                                                                               \
        (IT)->v_point[axis] = 0;                                               \
                                                                               \
        (IT)->location -= ((IT)->v_point_dimensions[axis] - 1) *               \
          (IT)->v_location_strides[axis];                                      \
      }                                                                        \
    }                                                                          \
  } while (0)

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Same as `rray_iterator`, but reports in two location spaces while only
// walking the point space once
struct rray_iterator2 {
  r_ssize index;
  r_ssize size;

  int v_point[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int v_point_dimensions[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
  int point_dimensionality;

  r_ssize location1;
  r_ssize v_location1_strides[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];

  r_ssize location2;
  r_ssize v_location2_strides[RRAY_MAX_DIMENSIONALITY];
};

static inline void rray_iterator2_init(
  struct rray_iterator2* it,
  r_ssize size,
  const int* v_point_dimensions,
  int point_dimensionality,
  const int* v_location1_dimensions,
  int location1_dimensionality,
  const int* v_location2_dimensions,
  int location2_dimensionality
) {
  check_max_dimensionality(point_dimensionality);

  it->index = 0;
  it->size = size;

  it->point_dimensionality = point_dimensionality;

  for (int i = 0; i < point_dimensionality; ++i) {
    it->v_point_dimensions[i] = v_point_dimensions[i];
  }
  memset(it->v_point, 0, sizeof(int) * point_dimensionality);

  rray__location_strides_init(
    it->v_location1_strides,
    v_point_dimensions,
    point_dimensionality,
    v_location1_dimensions,
    location1_dimensionality,
    "location1"
  );
  it->location1 = 0;

  rray__location_strides_init(
    it->v_location2_strides,
    v_point_dimensions,
    point_dimensionality,
    v_location2_dimensions,
    location2_dimensionality,
    "location2"
  );
  it->location2 = 0;
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------

#define RRAY_ITERATOR2_FOR_EACH(IT, INDEX, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, ...)          \
  do {                                                                         \
    while ((IT)->index != (IT)->size) {                                        \
      const r_ssize end = (IT)->index + (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];           \
      const r_ssize stride1 = (IT)->v_location1_strides[0];                    \
      const r_ssize stride2 = (IT)->v_location2_strides[0];                    \
      r_ssize LOCATION1 = (IT)->location1;                                     \
      r_ssize LOCATION2 = (IT)->location2;                                     \
      for (r_ssize INDEX = (IT)->index; INDEX != end; ++INDEX) {               \
        __VA_ARGS__                                                            \
        LOCATION1 += stride1;                                                  \
        LOCATION2 += stride2;                                                  \
      }                                                                        \
      (IT)->index += (IT)->v_point_dimensions[0];                              \
                                                                               \
      for (int axis = 1; axis < (IT)->point_dimensionality; ++axis) {          \
        ++(IT)->v_point[axis];                                                 \
                                                                               \
